### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/On_Agentic_Manipulation_Policies|On Agentic Manipulation Policies]]

![[assets/2611.09892_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.09892](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09892)

- **作者**: Chen Chen, Omid Bagherieh, Naveen Nikamanth Appiah Balaji, Jesse T. Gonzalez, Negin Heravi, Fahad Islam, Xueting Li, Jean-Baptiste Regli, Kejia Ren, Petr Švec, David J. Yoon, Matthias Müller...

- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Agent, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《On Agentic Manipulation Policies》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CaP-Gym。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Recent work on robot manipulation has focused on specialist policies trained on demonstrations. We show that generalist AI models can control manipulators more reliably than specialist policies such as vision-language-action models (VLAs) and world-action models (WAMs). We systematically analyze agentic manipulation policies (AMPs), in which a generalist AI model commands the manipulator directly, adapting its actions as the task unfolds. We present AMP1, a reference harness that runs unchanged across single-arm, bimanual, and mobile manipulation. AMP1 achieves state-of-the-art results on the RoboLab-120 and CaP-Gym benchmarks, with no demonstrations or task-specific training. On RoboLab-120, AMP1 reaches an 85.6\% success rate zero-shot, compared to 49.2\% and 42.9\% for leading VLA and WAM policies. The agent succeeds zero-shot in 104 of 108 real-world trials spanning twelve tasks and three robot instances, reducing failure rates by 20x and 15x relative to strong VLA and WAM baselines. The basic harness supplies no grasping, pushing, or placing skills; the agent develops its own strategies, such as searching for hidden objects, using objects as tools, and calibrating its cameras. With the addition of a persistent memory store, the agent can also learn from experience: an augmented harness with a memory representation that is carried across episodes lowers the failure rate on RoboDojo bimanual manipulation tasks by 4.6x relative to zero-shot deployment. Our analysis suggests that AMPs are substantially more general and robust than specialist manipulation policies, providing a promising foundation for robot manipulation.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Computer-Use Agents Are Embodied Generalists》归入 具身智能、大模型、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、大模型、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

In many areas of AI, general-purpose models have increasingly replaced handcrafted methods and specialized architectures. We argue that embodied AI is poised for a similar shift. Our key claim is that embodied tasks can be formulated as interaction with digital software. Specifically, we build software that renders an agent's view of a physical environment on a screen and provides controls for acting in it. A computer-use agent (CUA) can then perform embodied tasks through the same screenshots and keyboard and mouse actions that it uses to operate ordinary software. Using a single CUA design and changing only the software, our entries win first-, second-, third-, and sixth-place prizes across four ECCV workshop competitions, without any competition-specific agent tuning. These results suggest that the skills CUAs learn on software, such as visual grounding, precise clicking, and exploration, already carry over to embodied tasks, giving CUAs a far broader scope in embodied AI than previously realized. We therefore advocate that computer-use agents can be embodied generalists. Further, we advocate that, by following the training strategy that made computer-use agents general, embodied AI can converge on a single general model as well, ushering in a new era of general embodied AI. Website: https://pranjal2041.github.io/cua-embodied

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/SURE_Spatiotemporal_Uncertainty_Representation_for_Efficient_Vision-Language-Action_Learning|SURE:Spatiotemporal Uncertainty Representation for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Learning]]

![[assets/2611.09684_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.09684](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09684)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09684
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/SURE_Spatiotemporal_Uncertainty_Representation_for_Efficient_Vision-Language-Action_Learning|SURE:Spatiotemporal Uncertainty Representation for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Learning]]
- **作者**: Jingye Zhang, Choo Sin Wai, Dake Zhong, Zhifang Liu, Yuhong Zhang, Siyao Xiao, Guorui Song, Guocun Wang, Dongbin Zhang, Peng Jiao, Haoqian Wang
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.3（加权：具身智能 1.8，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《SURE:Spatiotemporal Uncertainty Representation for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Learning》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have become a promising paradigm for end-to-end robot control, but their effectiveness still depends heavily on large volumes of demonstration data. In manipulation tasks, this dependence is compounded by structured spatiotemporal uncertainty in the action space: multiple nearby actions or trajectories may successfully solve the same task, while semantically equivalent demonstrations often unfold at different speeds. Standard pointwise supervision treats each demonstrated action as an exact target, imposing an unnecessary learning burden under such uncertainty. We present SURE, a data-efficient VLA training framework that explicitly models both spatial and temporal uncertainty. SURE augments a VLA backbone with a lightweight transposed-convolutional probabilistic action decoder that captures spatial uncertainty through local correlations among neighboring action bins. It further introduces Soft-DTW alignment supervision to accommodate local timing variations between predicted and demonstrated action chunks. Together, these two components reduce sensitivity to demonstrator-specific poses and execution timing, enabling more data-efficient action learning. We evaluate SURE on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world manipulation tasks. Even with only 10% of the demonstration data or 20% of the training steps, SURE achieves manipulation success rates that match or surpass baseline VLAs.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Autonomous Particle Image Velocimetry Using Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning》归入 具身智能、强化学习、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、强化学习、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Particle image velocimetry (PIV) reconstructs a flow field from the motion of tracer particles but the reconstruction quality is bounded by how well the flow structure of interest is resolved in the images. The acquisition position is typically fixed by the operator before the measurement. We cast camera placement as a sequential decision-making problem and train a reinforcement learning agent to control a 6-DoF manipulator equipped with a camera, enabling it to locate, frame and resolve a hydrofoil wake in a water tunnel. The agent commands the camera pose and observes the scene only through the velocity field and uncertainty estimated from time-separated image pairs. It is trained entirely in a photorealistic simulation of our water tunnel, with wakes generated by Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) simulations of hydrofoils with varying profiles, positions and angles of attack. After zero-shot transfer to the real water tunnel, the policy reliably locates and frames the wakes of randomly placed hydrofoils. It resolves the wake at a higher resolution than a fixed camera view and with lower error in the measured flow statistics, in roughly half the time of a full scan and with far fewer image pairs.

</details>

![[assets/2611.09672_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.09672](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09672)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09672
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/ODEAction_From_Predicting_Chunks_to_Evolving_Actions|ODEAction: From Predicting Chunks to Evolving Actions]]
- **作者**: Qishen Wang, Pengxiang Ding, Shifeng Bao, Mingyang Sun, Hengtao Li, Yi Gao, Baohua Yin, Yang Liu, Jiajun Fu, Jiacheng Liu, Changjing He, Peiyang Li...
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO

- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人

- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《ODEAction: From Predicting Chunks to Evolving Actions》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：EBench, StarVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language-action (VLA) models typically generate actions in chunks, organizing continuous robot execution into separately predicted segments and thus introducing potential discontinuities at action-chunk transitions. We propose ODEAction, a chunk-free action generation paradigm that replaces action-chunk prediction with Continuous Action Evolution. Given visual observations, language instructions, and robot proprioceptive information, ODEAction learns an action transition field defined over action space, where a transition vector characterizes the evolution rate of the current action with respect to evolution time. During execution, the model takes the current action as its initial condition and integrates the learned field using an ordinary differential equation (ODE), recursively generating subsequent actions with explicit dependencies. ODEAction is implemented as an action head and does not depend on a specific vision-language backbone architecture. Experiments on LIBERO, EBench, RoboTwin, and real robots show that ODEAction improves both task performance and trajectory quality over matched action-chunking baselines, with particularly pronounced gains on fine manipulation tasks.

</details>

![[assets/2611.09605_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.09605](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09605)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09605
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/DKWBC_Decoupling_Motion_Tracking_and_Balancing_for_Humanoid_Whole-Body_Control|DKWBC: Decoupling Motion Tracking and Balancing for Humanoid Whole-Body Control]]
- **作者**: Dingkun Wei, Zehong Shen, Huaijin Pi, Xueyang Wang, Xing Zhu, Hujun Bao, Yujun Shen, Xiaowei Zhou
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人
- **相关性评分**: 2.8（加权：具身智能 1.5，机器人 1.3）
- **关联关键词**: Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《DKWBC: Decoupling Motion Tracking and Balancing for Humanoid Whole-Body Control》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Humanoid whole-body control requires a robot to track diverse reference motions while maintaining balance under gravity, torque limits, contact switching, and external disturbances. Existing reinforcement-learning-based trackers typically learn control outputs that jointly address motion tracking and balance. However, similar reference poses can correspond to substantially different dynamic demands, requiring a unified policy to learn motion-dependent control requirements alongside balance corrections. This can complicate learning and lead to high-frequency fluctuations in control outputs. We propose DKWBC, a dynamics-informed whole-body control framework that combines explicit model-based tracking with a learned balancing residual. Inspired by the model-based and environmental-wrench contributions to joint torques in floating-base dynamics, DKWBC uses inverse-dynamics feedforward and joint PD feedback to supply tracking torques, while a residual policy learns additional joint-torque corrections for balanced execution. By explicitly accounting for motion-dependent torque demands, this structure simplifies the policy's learning task. Experiments in simulation and on real hardware demonstrate that a single learned balancing policy generalizes across motion datasets while maintaining accurate motion tracking and balance across ground support, attached payloads, and physical interaction. Project page: https://robbyant-research.github.io/DKWBC/.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《DexDICE: Reconciling Safety and Performance via Dual-Expert Offline Imitation Learning》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DSRL, OSRL, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Ensuring high returns while providing strong safety guarantees in offline imitation learning (IL) is a fundamental challenge: existing approaches either rely on explicitly specified cost functions, which are difficult to design for complex real-world safety constraints, or require expert demonstrations that are both safe and high-performing, which are often too costly to collect. We introduce dual-expert imitation learning, which leverages two complementary data sources: (i) safety-compliant demonstrations that faithfully follow safety guidelines but may be suboptimal, and (ii) performance-oriented demonstrations that achieve high returns but may violate safety guidelines. To address this problem, we propose DexDICE (Dual-expert offline imitation learning via stationary DIstribution Correction Estimation). Instead of conventional distribution constraints, DexDICE combines DICE-style offline learning with safe support constraints, enabling agents to exploit high-return behaviors while remaining within safety-compliant regions. We prove that the ideal solution satisfying this constraint exactly has zero unsafe-region visitation with high probability. We evaluate DexDICE on a re-curated DSRL benchmark and validate it on a real-world mobile-robot navigation task trained directly from human teleoperation demonstrations, where it substantially outperforms baselines and remains robust to data scarcity and quality degradation.

</details>

《Can LLMs Debug Robots by Editing Their Weights?》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A language model can repair a robot policy's lexical instruction grounding by editing just 65 token-embedding rows. We bring weight-space programming to robot foundation policies: GPT-6 Astra reads weights and uses a CPU workspace and budgeted simulator feedback, without gradient updates or new demonstrations. Three independent sessions on $π_{0.5}$ all improve success on 765 held-out paraphrases; the best raises success from 86.3\% to 92.0\% (48 repairs, four regressions; exact paired $p=1.31\times10^{-10}$). Among the 335 instructions containing edited tokens, success rises from 74.3\% to 87.5\%; the other 430 episodes have zero outcome changes. Matched random-direction edits do not help. Full replacement using the editor's own lexicon explains much of the gain but produces more regressions; its direct difference from the editor is not significant at 0.05. Across 13 further robot sessions, the editor declines 12 candidates; the sole retained edit does not show a significant held-out gain. Separately, benchmark metadata lowers FastWAM success from 70.0\% to 48.6\%, while the evaluated VLA's aggregate rate is unchanged. The evidence supports precise lexical repair, not general acquisition of missing robot capabilities.

</details>

- **作者**: Boran Zhao, Yanbin Hu, Jun Ye, Zhenxian Hu, Yuxin Zhai, Jin Cui, Pengju Ren
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《HiMEM: Hippocampus-Inspired Memory Consolidation, Pruning, and Composition for Vision-Language-Action Models in 3D Scenes》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MAP-VLA, MemoryVLA, OpenVLA, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Memory plays a crucial role in long-horizon task execution and cross-task generalization for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models operating in complex 3D scenes. However, existing VLA memory mechanisms suffer from memory noise, particularly in complex 3D scenes, as stored episodic memories contain extensive redundant details about scene layouts, object configurations, and robot-specific states, which obscure generalizable manipulation principles and hinder cross-task transfer. To address this challenge, we first introduce a hippocampus-inspired memory consolidation mechanism that synthesizes extensive episodic experiences into a compact set of essential semantic memories, enabling VLA models to derive generalizable principles from practical experience. Second, we propose an episodic memory pruning mechanism that removes redundant episodic memories whose contents are captured by consolidated semantic memories, thereby increasing effective memory capacity and improving long-horizon task execution. Finally, to handle complex task scenarios, we develop a flexible memory composition mechanism that jointly leverages multiple episodic and semantic memories to enhance cross-scenario generalization. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus demonstrate the effectiveness of HiMEM across different VLA backbones, improving the average success rate of pi_0 from 91.88% to 98.20% on LIBERO.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We present Dejavu, a method that makes sim-to-real transfer of reinforcement learning policies in constrained Markov decision processes probably safe over an unbounded sequence of policy updates. An anytime valid test using per-decision importance sampling and a betting confidence sequence certifies each candidate trained in simulation before deployment using only trajectories collected by a safe baseline policy operating in production. Simulation quality does not affect the safety guarantee, only the production sample efficiency of the method. Finally, a summable per-test failure probability yields the lifetime valid property. Empirical results across three sim-to-real benchmarks support the theoretical work.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《MDF++: Visuo-Tactile World Action Model with End-to-end Representation Learning》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Recently, world-action models (WAMs) have emerged as a powerful framework for robot manipulation. By jointly modeling action generation and future observation prediction, WAMs can act and anticipate the outcomes of the actions. However, existing WAMs typically rely on frozen pretrained encoders trained with reconstruction, which may not produce representations well suited for robot control. In this paper, we tackle the problem of learning physicallygrounded, control-relevant representations for WAMs. We propose MDF++, a visuo-tactile world-action model that learns latent representations of multisensory observations end-to-end with a diffusion backbone. While naive joint training can lead to feature collapse, MDF++ introduces a multi-task learning strategy that leverages cross-modal correlations to learn task-relevant representations. We evaluate MDF++ on a suite of contact-rich manipulation tasks, including realworld bolt screwing and nut mating, and show that it outperforms WAMs built on frozen pretrained representations by up to 50%. Additional videos are available on our website: jointmdf.github.io.

</details>

《SUGARv2: Learning Diverse Visual Humanoid Loco-Manipulation Skills from Synthesized Interaction References》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Scaling up visual humanoid loco-manipulation skill acquisition requires reducing the human effort needed as tasks and spatial configurations expand, particularly in demonstration collection and task-specific training design. We present SUGARv2, a unified framework for learning diverse visual humanoid loco-manipulation skills through interaction reference synthesis and a shared training pipeline. To obtain diverse references at low cost, we adapt reusable task templates to different spatial configurations, synthesizing coordinated robot and object motions. To reduce task-specific reward design and training effort, we use reference-based imitation as a common learning objective across tasks. We first train privileged teachers to execute these references, then train visual policies through teacher distillation and reinforcement learning with the same reference-based mimic rewards, using diverse object instances and visual domain randomization to support visual generalization and sim-to-real transfer. Across five humanoid loco-manipulation tasks, SUGARv2 achieves a mean success rate of 91.0% in simulation. Deployment on a real humanoid robot further demonstrates successful sim-to-real transfer for whole-body manipulation. These results demonstrate the framework's potential for learning diverse visual humanoid loco-manipulation skills.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Foundation models increasingly guide robot actions, yet sustained autonomy requires efficient control and failure recovery. We introduce A2-MEAS, an embodied multi-agent framework built around code as a scaling interface. Learned correction codes combine corrective language, spatial evidence, execution history, and action proposals. MetaBrain provides training supervision and runtime guidance. A correction encoder and adapter absorb this knowledge into policy weights during training, while fresh codes adapt behavior with frozen weights at deployment. The action model, A2-WLA, adds motion-aware history and a latent world model to a vision-language-action policy. Surprisingly, this simple design with action pretraining and task specialization reaches 0.5755 Q-score on the 50-task BEHAVIOR 2026 public subset before online correction, 2.21x the 2025 winner's reported 0.2605 across different benchmark editions and training configurations. On all 100 BEHAVIOR 2026 public tasks, A2-MEAS achieves 0.6411 Q-score and 45.40% task success. On RoboTwin 2.0, A2-WLA achieves 93.98% average success, rising to 95.05% with the complete system. Across 12 real-robot tasks in five household settings, A2-WLA achieves 57.92% average success versus 26.67% for pi0.5. In long-horizon pour-over coffee preparation, a history-conditioned adapter raises post-failure completion at two filter-handling steps from 14% to 94% and from 8% to 82%, compared with A2-WLA retries. Our training infrastructure delivers 427.1 samples per second, 4.03x the throughput of a pi0.5 reference implementation on matched hardware. We will release the system, models, training scripts, and infrastructure to support efficient embodied learning.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/DobotTrack_Target-Grounded_Vision-Language-Action_Learning_for_Embodied_Person_Tracking|DobotTrack: Target-Grounded Vision-Language-Action Learning for Embodied Person Tracking]]

![[assets/2611.09041_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.09041](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09041)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09041
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/DobotTrack_Target-Grounded_Vision-Language-Action_Learning_for_Embodied_Person_Tracking|DobotTrack: Target-Grounded Vision-Language-Action Learning for Embodied Person Tracking]]
- **作者**: Shengjun Deng, Yinghan Zhang, Chengrui Wei, Liu Liang, Songyuan Liu, Pengkun Zheng, Yajun Zheng, Ying Zhang, Yuan Tian, Xi Li, Teng Wang
- **cs 子类**: cs.CV, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《DobotTrack: Target-Grounded Vision-Language-Action Learning for Embodied Person Tracking》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：EVT-Bench, OmTrackVLA, Real-World, TPT-Bench, TrackVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Language-guided embodied person tracking requires a mobile agent to identify a target from a referring description and consistently follow the target under egocentric observations. We present DobotTrack, a target-grounded vision-language-action (VLA) framework that integrates auxiliary grounding supervision with efficient temporal modeling. We systematically investigate five auxiliary tasks---person segmentation, bounding-box grounding, visual-spatial classification (VSC), future DINO feature prediction, and future-frame prediction---and adopt complementary mask and bounding-box supervision to enhance target delineation and localization. We introduce Q-identity, a target-conditioned historical visual compression module that leverages target-grounding predictions to selectively preserve identity-discriminative appearance features and spatial cues in compact historical tokens. On EVT-Bench, our 0.8B-parameter model achieves an average success rate of 77.9%, exceeding OmTrackVLA by 16.9 percentage points across the three tasks. We further construct a real-world person-tracking dataset collected using a quadruped robot, comprising 126 episodes and 596,323 mask-annotated frames. The target masks enable automatic derivation of bounding boxes and visual-spatial labels encoding horizontal position and apparent image scale, providing additional grounding supervision without separate manual annotation of these labels. Together, our framework and annotation pipeline support learning target-grounded tracking policies from simulated and real-world data. Code is available at https://github.com/Dobot-Team/dobot_track.

</details>

![[assets/2611.09036_figure.png|800]]

- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《ToolDex: An Ego-Exo Human Demonstration Dataset for Creative Physical Tool Use》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：PhysToolBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Creative tool use is a fundamental human capability and an important step toward general-purpose embodied intelligence. It requires agents to reason about object affordances, physical constraints, and manipulation strategies, especially when standard tools are unavailable. However, progress in this area is limited by the lack of real-world datasets that capture human tool-use behavior with sufficient scale, fidelity, and diversity. Existing benchmarks are often simulation-based, focus on simplified interactions, or lack detailed observations and annotations. We introduce ToolDex, a large-scale egocentric-exocentric (ego-exo) human demonstration dataset for creative physical tool use. ToolDex captures scenarios in which humans select, adapt, and repurpose available objects as tools. Using synchronized egocentric cameras and eight exocentric cameras, the dataset provides high-fidelity multi-view observations and rich annotations for hands, tools, target objects, and their deformable interactions. Beyond data collection, we establish a benchmark suite characterizing three core capabilities: physical tool-use reasoning, sparse-view 3D and 4D reconstruction, and robot policy learning. ToolDex provides a foundation for studying human tool-use behavior and developing embodied agents that can adaptively use tools in complex real-world environments. Dataset visualizations and benchmarking results are available at https://tooluse-dataset.github.io/.

</details>

![[assets/2611.09005_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.09005](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09005)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09005
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/FASTFUNNELS_Agile_Multi_Robot_Navigation_in_Narrow_Spaces_via_Adaptive_Dynamic_Funnels|FASTFUNNELS: Agile Multi Robot Navigation in Narrow Spaces via Adaptive Dynamic Funnels]]

- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 强化学习, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《FASTFUNNELS: Agile Multi Robot Navigation in Narrow Spaces via Adaptive Dynamic Funnels》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；实时应用对效率提出要求；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MARL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We focus on the problem of high-speed multi-robot navigation in cluttered spaces with tight spatial constraints. A key challenge is balancing safety with agility, as existing methods often trade one for the other (slowing down near a bottleneck to avoid collisions versus failing to swerve in time at high speeds), especially for car-like systems. Our approach belongs to the family of formation control methods, in which Ackermann-steering agents coordinate while navigating tight spaces. However, current formation control techniques rely on rigid, pre-defined shapes and transitions that depend on initial conditions, the environment, and vehicle dynamics, and are impractical to maintain at high speeds. For example, agents passing through a small gap must first form a square, transform into a line using analytical equations, and then return to a square. In contrast, our approach is inspired by a droplet of water that continuously adapts its shape to the space it flows through without slowing down. We propose a hierarchical algorithm in which, at the high level, a reinforcement learning policy produces a dynamic, blob-like elliptical patch that morphs its shape in real time, squeezing, stretching, and orienting itself around obstacles and through tight spaces. At the low level, the robots are controlled via distributed model predictive control (DMPC) to stay inside the patch, which acts as a soft forcing function, while avoiding collisions with each other. We evaluate our approach, FastFunnels, on simulated F1TENTH racing cars across 14 scenarios with N=1 to N=6 robots, averaged over 20 trials each. FastFunnels achieves an 89% completion rate at 7.73 m/s, while all baselines either fail to complete the track or succeed only at very low speeds (0.07-1.79 m/s, about 40% completion rate).

</details>

- **作者**: Takayuki Nishio

#### 研究背景与动机

《VLA Handover: Failure-Prediction-Based Switching from On-Robot to Server VLA Models》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AsyncVLA, CloudEdgeVLA, ComVLA, L-VLA, S-VLA, SmolVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large vision-language-action (VLA) models generalize well but are difficult to run in real time on a robot, and small VLAs that run there fail more often. Remote inference over beyond-5G and 6G networks lets a robot use a large VLA on a server, but querying the server for every action chunk limits the number of robots per server. We propose VLA handover: the robot runs a small VLA (S-VLA) and switches to a large VLA (L-VLA) on a server only when the S-VLA is likely to fail. We formulate the switching as minimizing the request cost under a required success probability and, since this problem cannot be solved directly, propose a heuristic framework that retrains neither VLA. In simulation with SmolVLA and $π_{0.5}$ on three of the four LIBERO suites, VLA handover raised the success rate by 11-22 percentage points over the S-VLA alone and sent 49-77% fewer requests than server-only control at a 2-4 percentage-point lower success rate, and its completion time grew less with the network latency.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We present RevoSim, a scalable and high-fidelity multimodal tactile simulator for the BrainCo Revo3 hand that jointly models 285 piezoresistive taxels and five visuotactile fingertips. RevoSim efficiently generates synchronized pressure, depth, RGB, and marker-displacement observations; on a single RTX 5090, its depth-conditioned marker-displacement configuration supports up to 4,096 parallel environments. We train in-hand manipulation and grasping policies entirely in simulation, establishing a foundation for future sim-to-real transfer. Project page: https://braincotech.github.io/RevoSim-web/.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/PRISM_Primitive_Routing_via_In-context_Skill_Mixing_for_Retentive_VLA_Adaptation|PRISM: Primitive Routing via In-context Skill Mixing for Retentive VLA Adaptation]]

![[assets/2611.08917_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.08917](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08917)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08917
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/PRISM_Primitive_Routing_via_In-context_Skill_Mixing_for_Retentive_VLA_Adaptation|PRISM: Primitive Routing via In-context Skill Mixing for Retentive VLA Adaptation]]
- **作者**: Jiachen Tao, Gengyu Zhang, Haoxuan Wang, Bing Liu, Yan Yan
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 2.4（加权：具身智能 1.8，大模型 0.1，机器人 0.5）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《PRISM: Primitive Routing via In-context Skill Mixing for Retentive VLA Adaptation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AtomicVLA, SkillVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models promise a single language-conditioned policy adaptable to many manipulation behaviors, yet deployment additionally requires skill retention and long-horizon composition: a policy must retain previously acquired skills and reuse them inside composite tasks whose behavior changes across phases. The standard recipe absorbs every new task into one shared update, which provides neither a parameter-level locus for reusable skills nor a mechanism for invoking each skill along the trajectory. We observe that this missing structure is latent rather than absent: per-skill low-rank updates of a pretrained VLA occupy a robustly distinguishable, low-dimensional subspace, and a single composite-task LoRA concentrates roughly half of its Frobenius energy in this subspace, against a near-zero matched random-rotation control. Building on this geometry, we introduce PRISM, which represents reusable skills as parameter-level primitives: each skill family is stored as a dedicated low-rank action-expert adapter in a primitive bank, primitives are augmented with skill-specific low-rank residuals and composed at inference by a per-chunk VLM-conditioned router that reads the prefix representation to track task progress, and the router is supervised by per-chunk labels from a deterministic, task-conditioned, action-derived oracle. During composite training, the vision encoder, the language LoRA, the skill residuals, the router, and the shared action projections and time MLP are updated; every primitive is held frozen, so the base per-skill action-expert adapters are preserved by construction. On RoboCasa365, PRISM achieves 56.43% atomic-skill success and reaches 20.00% on long-horizon composite tasks, while a standard $π_{0.5}$-LoRA two-stage fine-tune reaches 17.86% atomic and 17.50% composite success. We additionally validate PRISM on an AgileX PIPER real-robot setup.

</details>

- **作者**: Sudhanshu Srivastava, Faisal Aqlan
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO

#### 研究背景与动机

《Localizing and Editing the Goal Representation of a Vision-Language-Action Model》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕论文提出的建模、算法或系统设计进行实验验证。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Operators can state safety constraints for robots as prohibitions such as "do not put the bowl on the stove". We study whether OpenVLA follows such prohibitions and where it represents the goal it executes. The 10 tasks of LIBEROGoal share one scene, so we can label the goal that each rollout executes. We combine closed-loop rollouts with activation patching, linear probes, activation steering and runtime monitors. On LIBERO-Goal, negation had no detectable effect on which task OpenVLA executed. The model executed the forbidden task in 79% of negated rollouts and completed 78% of plain tasks. A goal probe gives similar read-outs under "not X" and "X". Activation patching shows that the first action depends mostly on the instruction tokens before layer 12 and on the last prompt token from layer 16 onward. A fixed steering vector at the instruction tokens at layer 8 redirected 12 of 18 rollouts. Fixed vectors and prefill patches at layers 12 to 20 redirected none of 158. From layer 16, only patches at every decoded action token redirected the robot (15 of 18 rollouts at layer 24). When the instruction changes during a rollout, the layer-32 goal read-out detects non-compliance better than failure detectors, and an external probe on image and instruction features performs comparably. These results support enforcing prohibitions outside the instruction and monitoring goal read-outs against the specification. Goal edits in OpenVLA should act before layer 12 or patch every action token.

</details>

![[assets/2611.08857_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Nico Bohlinger, Jan Peters
- **cs 子类**: cs.LG, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 强化学习, 世界模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《STRIDE: Learning Humanoid Locomotion in Minutes with Acceleration-Based Rewards》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Learning locomotion directly on a humanoid robot is a promising direction to avoid the sim-to-real gap. However, locomotion rewards in Reinforcement Learning (RL) typically track the base velocity, which cannot be measured onboard without an external motion capture system or well-calibrated state estimator. This is why we introduce \method{}, which learns humanoid locomotion from minutes of samples with a reward that only requires the onboard Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU). Instead of tracking the base velocity, STRIDE rewards the robot for following a commanded acceleration profile. To learn efficiently from this reward, we use the off-policy RL algorithm XQC and combine a high gain setting for the joint position controllers with left-right symmetry augmentation. In simulation, STRIDE learns to walk on the Unitree G1 and Booster T1 humanoids within 14 minutes of samples. Compared to learning without these two components, it reduces the falls during training by 81%, while without them the robot learns only in over an hour.

</details>

- **作者**: Sungwon Moon, Minji Kim, Hyemin Boo, Daeun Song, Jiyoung Lee
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人

#### 研究背景与动机

《ActPrune: Action-Grounded Visual Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenVLA, SpecPrune-VLA, VLABench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Vision-language-action (VLA) models perform versatile manipulation tasks from language instructions, but their inference cost limits how fast a robot can respond in closed-loop control. Visual token pruning reduces this cost; however, prior methods estimate token redundancy from encoded visual embeddings or attention, only after the visual encoder has processed the entire observation. We propose \ours, a training-free token pruning method that moves the pruning decision ahead of the vision encoder by grounding it in a geometric prior derived from the robot's own action. The action chunk predicted at the previous control step is already available before the current observation is encoded, and it specifies the end-effector and camera motion between consecutive observations. The same action affects the two standard camera views asymmetrically: in the fixed third-person view, \ours retains patches along the projected end-effector trajectory together with semantic anchors, while in the wrist view, it prunes patches that remain unchanged after compensating for the action-induced camera motion. Extensive experiments on two simulation environments (LIBERO and VLABench) and four real-robot tasks demonstrate that \ours achieves up to 1.61 $\times$ speedup and 59.5\% FLOPs reduction with negligible degradation in success rates.

</details>

![[assets/2611.08698_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.08698](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08698)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08698

- **作者**: Luca Macesanu, Tianluo Zhang, Aditya Patil, Archit Sharma, Kevin Angers, Christopher M. Clark, Chen Feng, Juexiao Zhang
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《VICTR: Value-Informed In-Context Learning for Vision-Language-Action Models》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：IC-VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Recent advances in robot foundation models have highlighted the potential of in-context learning (ICL) for adapting robot behavior from demonstrations without further fine-tuning. Previous work has used retrieval-based post-training to equip pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models with the capability for ICL by teaching them to condition action predictions on retrieved visually-relevant demonstration chunks. However, the effectiveness of retrieval hinges on selecting context relevant to the current execution stage, which visual similarity alone may not capture. We introduce VICTR, a framework enabling in-context learning in VLAs via progress-informed context retrieval built around an In-Context Value Function Estimator (IC-VFE). IC-VFE conditions on a reference demonstration video, a language instruction and the current observation to estimate task-relative progress. By conditioning progress estimation on a reference demonstration, IC-VFE can align the current state with a demonstrated execution even for tasks unseen during training. VICTR uses the estimated progress to select demonstration chunks at corresponding stages of execution and supplies them to a $π_{0.5}$ policy post-trained for in-context action generation. On unseen out-of-domain real-world manipulation tasks, VICTR raises the success rate from 6.2% for a fine-tuned $π_{0.5}$ baseline to 23.8% and more than doubles the task progress rate, from 28.9% to 55.5%. Project page with code, data and videos: https://ai4ce.github.io/VICTR/

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/ForPC_Force-Aware_Whole-Body_Control_for_Precise_Humanoid_Cart_Pushing|ForPC: Force-Aware Whole-Body Control for Precise Humanoid Cart Pushing]]

![[assets/2611.08664_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.08664](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08664)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.08664

- **作者**: ZhenYu Zou, Xing Liu, ShiChuang Wang, YunLong Dong, LiNan Deng, Feng Hua, Jun Wan, Cheng Cheng
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 强化学习

- **关联关键词**: Robotics, RL, Systems

#### 研究背景与动机

《ForPC: Force-Aware Whole-Body Control for Precise Humanoid Cart Pushing》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World, WholeBodyVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Humanoid robots are expected to take over strenuous material handling in manufacturing, logistics, and healthcare, where operating existing wheeled carts would let them move heavy loads without modifying the workplace. However, humanoid cart pushing remains challenging because the bimanual grasp closes a force-coupled loop in which the contact wrenches that drive the cart also disturb the robot's balance, and payload changes alter the coupled dynamics. We propose ForPC, a force-aware whole-body control framework that regulates cart velocity through two-stage reinforcement learning. A locomotion prior is first trained under varied hand loads and upper-body postures to provide balance support. A force latent and a state latent are then inferred from onboard observations, without external force sensors, and fed to a residual policy that coordinates bounded arm, waist, and leg adjustments with base-velocity commands. Multi-objective rewards penalize bimanual internal opposition, grasp slip, and actuator saturation, encouraging the policy to apply contact force on demand without human demonstrations. Sim-to-sim experiments show that ForPC reduces cart-velocity RMSE by up to 68.2% relative to a locomotion-only baseline (Loco-only) and cross-track RMSE by up to 74.2% on the multi-bend route. Trained solely in simulation, the policy also transfers to a physical Unitree G1 without real-world training or fine-tuning and, with no payload-specific adjustment, pushes a cart carrying up to 30 kg at 0.5 m/s. These results suggest that humanoid robots could eventually replace humans for efficient material handling in daily life.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Meituan-Robotics-0: A Vision-Language-Action Foundation Model for Desktop Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AgiBot-World, Real-World, StarVLA, VQA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We present Meituan-Robotics-0 (MR0), a vision-language-action foundation model and embodied-agent system for stationary-base desktop manipulation. MR0 combines 147 million vision-language samples with 15,060 hours of curated robot trajectories spanning 12 embodiments. A three-stage training recipe aligns multimodal representations with robot behavior through discrete action-token pre-training, transfers these priors to continuous cross-embodiment control through flow-matching mid-training, and adapts the policy through real-world post-training. We introduce Rollback, which pairs replaced policy segments with human corrections to provide positive and negative supervision for CFG-DAgger without value learning or advantage labeling. A unified end-effector interface supports heterogeneous embodiments, while asynchronous action-chunk merging maintains smooth execution under variable inference latency. An event-driven agent maintains persistent task events and reassesses plans when progress or feasibility changes, enabling cross-task retrieval and conditional task resumption. MR0 achieves 93.6% success on RoboTwin 2.0 and ranks fifth in the RoboDojo VLA track. Under matched HG-DAgger post-training, MR0 achieves higher average success rate and progress score than $π_{0.5}$ across three real-robot tasks. On Ethernet insertion, CFG-DAgger trained with Rollback data reaches 70% success and a progress score of 0.90. Qualitative agent deployments demonstrate cross-task event retrieval and recovery from temporarily unsatisfied task conditions.

</details>

《Open-HumanWAM: An Open Recipe for Scaling Human and Robot Experience for Embodied Foundation Models》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：HumanNet, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Egocentric human video offers a scalable source of manipulation priors for robot learning, yet translating raw data scale into generalizable robot policies demands rigorous data curation and training recipes. We introduce Open-HumanWAM, the first fully open recipe for scaling egocentric pretraining to 50,000 hours, anchored by Open-HumanWAM-50K---a curated egocentric video corpus densely annotated with hand motion and natural language. Leveraging this corpus, we systematically investigate pure human pretraining via closed-loop simulation evaluations and uncover an empirical log-linear scaling law linking pretraining data hours to downstream robot success rates. Grounded in these findings, we develop HumanWAM-Vanilla, a world--action model pretrained exclusively on 50k hours of egocentric human video. Upon robot post-training, HumanWAM-Vanilla reaches 92.08% success on RoboTwin 2.0 Clean2Clean and 68.88% on Clean2Random, alongside a 14.17% mean success rate and a 19.00 mean progress score on RoboDojo. To push the capability frontier further, we explore joint human--robot pretraining with HumanWAM-Goal, a visual-goal-conditioned world--action model. Hybrid data scaling unlocks broad zero-shot generalization across both simulation and physical benchmarks, propelling post-trained RoboTwin 2.0 performance to new state-of-the-art levels: 92.70% on Clean2Clean and a substantial leap to 84.60% on Clean2Random. On physical robots, HumanWAM-Goal further exhibits emergent embodied in-context learning, dynamically adapting its manipulation behavior to unseen visual goals without parameter updates. To support reproducible scaling research in embodied AI, we open-source the Open-HumanWAM-50K corpus, data curation and pretraining pipelines, alongside the pretrained and post-trained HumanWAM checkpoints, inference code, and simulation evaluation suites.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Compaction, decay and reinforcement in a two-tier conversational memory: a paired evaluation against retrieval-augmented generation》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computation and Language 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：LongMemEval。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A conversational agent that remembers has to decide what to keep. Retrieval-augmented generation keeps the transcript and searches it; the alternative is to compact each turn into atomic facts and search those. We compare the two on LoCoMo, with both arms answering all 1,985 questions in one window, against the same stores, with the same generator and judge, and both predictions on one row, which licenses McNemar's exact test on the 512 questions where they disagree. On the four categories comparable work reports, compaction scores 71.4 per cent against 64.6, a lead of 6.8 points (p = 5.5e-08) at 34 per cent fewer prompt tokens. On all five, including the adversarial set comparable work drops, the lead falls to 2.0 and stops being significant, because the baseline leads adversarial by 14.6. That loss has a structure. Our lead falls as the reference answer demands more source wording, 9.6 points ahead at one or two reference words and 3.1 behind past eight, with token overlap moving the same way, so the judge is not the cause. Compaction keeps what a fact asserts and discards how it was said, so it wins where the answer is a value and loses where it is a phrasing. We also report four negative results with their power calculations.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/When_Instruction_Files_Go_Stale_Documentation_Debt_and_Hidden_Cost_in_Coding_Agents|When Instruction Files Go Stale: Documentation Debt and Hidden Cost in Coding Agents]]

![[assets/2611.09802_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.09802](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09802)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.09802
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/When_Instruction_Files_Go_Stale_Documentation_Debt_and_Hidden_Cost_in_Coding_Agents|When Instruction Files Go Stale: Documentation Debt and Hidden Cost in Coding Agents]]
- **作者**: Tural Mehtiyev, Biruk Tadesse, Lawrence Arkoh, Wesley Assunção
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.SE
- **归属领域**: 大模型
- **相关领域**: 大模型

- **关联关键词**: LLM, Agent

#### 研究背景与动机

《When Instruction Files Go Stale: Documentation Debt and Hidden Cost in Coding Agents》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

LLM-based coding agents such as Claude Code are guided by repository instruction files (also called context files or agent configuration files) like AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md, which are read in every agent session. As any documentation artifact, these files can fall behind the code and become stale (e.g., a test folder moves, but the file still points to the old one). In technical debt terms, such a stale instruction is documentation debt, and its interest is the cost it adds to every later agent session. Evaluations that check only task success can miss this cost, and the stale file can persist. In this study, we ask what leads agents to act on a stale instruction, how it affects their behavior and at what cost, and how they recover. We study three real-world stale instructions from active open-source repositories in depth, each a different kind of guidance: a test command, a code location, and a procedure for adding a component. Agents fix 22 maintenance tasks under three versions of each instruction (left stale, corrected to the current path, and removed), with the code and the task held fixed, and we analyze 732 trajectories of two models at the level of individual tool calls. We find that agents follow stale guidance when it is relevant to their tasks, sometimes even when correct information is available elsewhere. On the stale test command, Claude Sonnet 5.5 needed about two extra tool calls per run, a third more than with the corrected command; in the other cases, we could not detect a difference. Although agents generally recovered from the immediate failures, none repaired the underlying instruction file. Correcting the instructions eliminated operations on obsolete paths, while removing them prevented most. Our findings show that task success alone can hide the costs of documentation debt, highlighting the need to evaluate agent behavior beyond final outcomes.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Strategic_Competition_and_Convergence_in_LLM_Leaderboards|Strategic Competition and Convergence in LLM Leaderboards]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《Strategic Competition and Convergence in LLM Leaderboards》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computers and Society 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Leaderboards are a popular way to compare large language model (LLM) capabilities and can influence how model developers invest resources. We ask when competition on leaderboards leads developers to produce models with similar capability profiles, which we call monoculture, or similar performance levels. We study this by formulating leaderboard competition as a game in which model developers invest resources across capabilities to maximize their win rate. We show that similar production functions can induce similar capability profiles, while comparative advantage can sustain distinct capability profiles. We then study convergence in performance levels and show that diminishing returns can lead to an increasingly crowded competitive frontier. Using data from LM Arena and OpenRouter, we find that developers improve more in categories where they lag competitors, while still retaining category-specific advantages. At the same time, we find that the frontier of models becomes increasingly crowded, alongside increasingly diversified model usage patterns. Our results suggest that increasing competition does not need to produce interchangeable models, but can instead sustain competitive models with distinct strengths.

</details>

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large language model (LLM) agents coordinate through conversation, and the same message can be phrased in simpler or more complex language, bluntly or politely. We test whether such differences change how other agents judge and act, using the LURK Werewolf framework, which separates each utterance's speech-act predicate from its natural-language realization. We rewrite only the realization, check that the game content is preserved, and replay each game day from saved checkpoints against a re-sampled control. We vary linguistic range consistently across a speaker's messages and politeness strategy per utterance. Across 30 games and 4,335 replays, neither manipulation changes the trust other agents place in the speaker, and no effect on votes or night actions survives correction for multiple comparisons; the largest uncorrected effect is that shifting range away from a neutral paraphrase changes about 4 percentage points more of the other players' votes. In natural play, 88% of face-threatening speech is bald on-record regardless of role.

</details>

《MemoryDojo: Benchmarking Poisoning Through LLM Agent Memory》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：LongMemEval, MPBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

LLM agents benefit from using memory systems to improve performance on long-horizon, multi-session tasks. However, memory introduces a critical attack surface. Malicious instructions encountered in one session may be stored and propagated through memory before being retrieved and acted upon in later sessions. Because memory systems differ in how they process and retrieve information, the downstream effects of poisoning can vary significantly across systems. To quantify the effects of poisoning across different memory architectures, we introduce MemoryDojo, a benchmark for tracing the lifecycle of poisoned information through agent memory and measuring its influence on subsequent agent behavior. MemoryDojo is easily extensible, consists of 155 multi-session long-horizon trajectories covering four domains, and supports five different memory systems. Through our evaluation, we find that all memory systems are susceptible to memory poisoning, with substantial variation in downstream effects. We further propose a memory-aware poison optimization process that increases the poisoning efficacy, highlighting differences between memory architectures. We hope that MemoryDojo will support research on understanding and mitigating agentic memory risks.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CURL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

The task of weakly supervised video anomaly detection is challenging. Existing approaches mainly model video frame features and determine whether anomalies are present based on their magnitudes. In this work, we propose a novel method that incorporates external knowledge into training to improve model performance, robustness, and generalization. We leverage textual prompts and a large language model (LLM) to establish prior information for the specific task, and employ dictionary learning to normalize and compress this information. Moreover, during experiments, we observed inconsistencies in the textual descriptions extracted from similar video frames by image caption models. To address this, we devise a method that leverages an LLM and auxiliary information to ensure consistency in the textual descriptions of similar frames within a video. Finally, we introduce a feature processing approach that incorporates prior knowledge and cross-modal attention mechanisms. This approach learns the relationships and characteristics among different modalities to construct more accurate and reliable feature representations, thereby improving the accuracy and stability of downstream tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that our method surpasses other state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Nebula: Distributed Position-Independent Caching for LLM Serving》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Distributed, Parallel, and Cluster Computing 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Modern LLM workflows, such as retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and LLM agents, build long prompts from multiple independent text segments, making prefill a major source of computation. Reusing KV caches is therefore important for reducing serving costs and latency: distributed caching systems spread KV cache across a cluster to increase aggregate capacity, while position-independent caching (PIC) enables reuse of recurring segments under different prefixes. However, existing systems distribute KV caches but still rely on prefix caching (PC), while existing PIC methods enable position-independent reuse without cluster-wide resource management. We present Nebula, the first distributed LLM serving framework to treat PIC segments as first-class citizens, enabling remote PIC reuse and parallel prefill. To track PIC's fine-grained, asynchronous state changes, Nebula combines optimistic accounting with event-driven updates to provide a timely and truthful cluster view for cache management and prefill scheduling. To accommodate diverse reuse patterns across workloads, Nebula leverages cluster-wide ghost-hit feedback to automatically tune the allocation of shared cache capacity between PC and PIC. Finally, Nebula incorporates queueing, computation, and KV transfer costs into a unified prefill completion time model to guide elastic parallelism adjustment and locality-aware compute placement. Across four models and four real-world RAG and agentic workloads, Nebula reduces median TTFT by an average of 61.4% compared with the strongest existing baseline and recovers an average of 76.1% of the tokens missed by prefix caching.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《ROAM: Retrieval-Augmented Optimization for Feasible Multi-Day Itinerary Planning》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) can draft plausible travel itineraries, but the plans they produce are frequently infeasible: attractions are grouped into days without regard for geography, opening hours are ignored, and a single generation pass cannot reliably organize a multi-day trip. We present ROAM, a retrieval-augmented agentic framework for spontaneous multi-day itinerary planning that couples semantic preference retrieval with explicit spatiotemporal optimization. ROAM embeds the natural-language request, retrieves a compact candidate set from a curated point-of-interest (POI) database, partitions candidates into day groups through maximal-clique analysis and K-means, orders each day with a quality-biased simulated-annealing TSP solver, and repairs hard opening-hour violations with greedy exchange. A spatiotemporal retrieval layer injects weather forecasts, real road distances and POI operating hours into the prompt, after which the LLM renders a structured, map-ready itinerary; an interactive ReAct-style replanning agent then supports natural-language edits while re-checking feasibility. Across four Chinese cities and 510 POIs, ROAM produces 27.5 POIs per three-day plan versus 19.3 for a bare LLM and 17.0 for a retrieval-only baseline (+42% and +62%), while reducing route dispersion from 2,101 to 677 (3.1x). Ablations show that spatial optimization is the dominant driver of route efficiency and that hard time-window repair prevents roughly 30% of attractions from violating operating hours.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Large Language Model Consensus and Corpus-Adaptive Topic Modeling for Classifying and Mapping Patent Innovation》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Artificial Intelligence 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Patent analytics increasingly relies on transformer representations and large language models (LLMs), yet linking conceptual classification, topic formation and interpretation requires explicit decisions about model roles and the evidence retained at each stage. In this design-oriented study, we propose and demonstrate a patent analytics pipeline that links these tasks and keeps each decision open to audit. Three LLMs separately classify each patent by novelty (radical or incremental) and innovation type (product, process, service). Radical and innovation-type labels each require two supporting votes, with Incremental used as the fallback novelty label. Topics are discovered by clustering PatentSBERTa embeddings, with parameter ranges scaled to corpus size. A patent-specific stopword list is applied only when topics are represented with class-based term frequency--inverse document frequency (c-TF-IDF), and a second embedding model supports inter-topic similarity, hierarchical refinement and visualization. An LLM then labels each topic from its ranked keywords and representative patents. We demonstrate the pipeline on 30,964 public-sector-related patents retrieved from Lens.org. Across five category corpora ranging from 591 to 29,215 patents, the pipeline produced 74 category-specific topic instances with a macro-average document coverage of 98.1% and topic diversity of 0.900--1.000, and fewer than 1% of classification outputs returned UNKNOWN. Because every model vote and the evidence behind each topic label are retained, analysts can audit and revise the outputs. The demonstration establishes the feasibility and traceability of the pipeline; testing its labels against expert coding and benchmarking it against alternative pipelines are identified as the next steps. The pipeline offers a reusable basis for LLM-assisted patent analysis in technology intelligence.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Authorization Drift in LLM Agents: Evaluating Boundary Compliance in Multi-Step Tool Use》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Cryptography and Security 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Agent-SafetyBench, AgentBench, AppWorld, EU-Agent-Bench, ToolBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

LLM agents can exceed their authorization boundaries while pursuing user goals without an adversary. We formalize this phenomenon as authorization drift across data, tool, scope, and time, and introduce the Authorization Drift Benchmark (ADB): 648 samples across seven domains, each pairing a sufficient compliant path with an attractive unauthorized shortcut. To ensure executable tasks, tempting shortcuts, and feasible compliant paths, Staged Execution-Reflective Synthesis (SERS) grounds benchmark validation in real agent rollouts rather than LLM self-critique. Across 13 LLMs and three frameworks (25,207 trajectories), greater capability accompanies greater drift, with frontier-capable models exhibiting drift rates of 0.43 to 0.61. Yet 86.18% of tasks admit an observed clean-success trajectory, showing that drift is avoidable. Drift is persistent and dominated by early data over-collection; apparent improvements under agentic scaffolds coincide with collapsing clean-completion performance. To understand potential harms to individuals and society, we map drift points to legal provisions across four jurisdictions, connecting overreach to privacy and information-security concerns. Data minimisation is the most frequently implicated provision, and 93.5% of legally grounded drifting runs map to multiple jurisdictions, making the potential consequences of authorization drift more interpretable.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/AlienBench_A_Benchmark_of_Structural_Novelty_in_Language_Model_Outputs|AlienBench: A Benchmark of Structural Novelty in Language Model Outputs]]

![[assets/2611.09094_first_page.png|800]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《AlienBench: A Benchmark of Structural Novelty in Language Model Outputs》归入 大模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Artificial Intelligence 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AlienBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for creative tasks such as writing, design, and idea generation. For these uses, it matters whether a model's ideas depart from familiar examples or stay close to them. Novelty is one standard measure of creativity, and current creativity evaluations ask human experts or LLM judges to rate how novel an output is. However, these ratings have no fixed reference for what counts as novel (or, in other terms, departure from the familiar). Measuring this departure requires a task with known familiar defaults. The structured imagination task from cognitive psychology is such a task. In this creativity test, people imagine a creature from another planet. The departure from features of Earth animals, such as bilateral symmetry and legs as mode of locomotion, is used as measure of creativity. We introduce AlienBench, a benchmark that adapts the structured imagination task to language models. Models describe alien creatures, and a panel of three LLM judges extracts ten biological features from the description and labels them as either Earth-typical or a departure. The sum of feature departures is the score of a description, from 0 to 10. Each model describes alien creatures under six prompt conditions that vary the framing of the task and the planetary environment. Each model generates 30 descriptions per condition (180 in total), and its overall score is the mean across these descriptions. We evaluate 10 language models, from frontier to open-weight models. Overall scores range from 3.28 (GPT-6 Luna) to 6.55 (Ministral 3 14B). Interestingly, open-weight models score higher than proprietary models in this task (5.40 vs. 4.58). Under the baseline prompt, the models depart from Earth defaults more often on average than 100 human participants on every feature. We release all prompts, generations, judge outputs, and scores.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Existing multilingual retrieval-augmented generation relies heavily on learned retrievers and similarity-based ranking from multilingual collections, which are vulnerable to cross-lingual retrieval errors and indexing overhead. In this paper, we explore retriever-free multilingual search, where the model directly interacts with raw corpora through executable shell commands. This paradigm shifts the bottleneck to multilingual search action generation, where exact search is sensitive to entity surface forms and outcome-only supervision provides limited guidance for multi-turn search. To address these challenges, we propose MARS, a multilingual search agent in direct corpus interaction (DCI) with reinforcement learning (RL). MARS introduces corpus-native entity grounding to recover reliable lexical anchors from corpus evidence, mitigating failure to retrieve evidence. Meanwhile, we present a redundancy-aware search reward to encourage concise and effective search trajectories, addressing training collapse in RL of multilingual scenarios. Experiments show that \ours~consistently outperforms strong retrieval-based and DCI baselines, while demonstrating robust cross-lingual generalization and substantially reducing redundant corpus interactions.

</details>
